## Current implemented curriculum — November 17, 2026

The source extends the boot and storage progression with two PXE lessons
and two iSCSI lessons. Current totals are 115 CCNA, 154 LPIC-1, 170 LPIC-2
and 80 Bash across 210 units. The first installed iSCSI review verifies
this 519-lab checkpoint.
The remaining scope includes broader storage hardware and firmware.
[Readiness evidence](CHECKPOINT.md) · [Scope reconciliation](REMAINING_SCOPE_RECONCILIATION.md)

## Every current objective and its remaining practical assignment

Each row is the recorded broader task still to implement, including when related
authored work already exists. Prerequisites, difficulty and required capabilities
remain in [CURRICULUM.csv](CURRICULUM.csv). The detailed remaining column of
SKILL_COVERAGE.csv is authoritative for each individual child and named tool.
An assignment may need several distinct activities; one row is not an arbitrary
one-lab quota. Physical/kernel/cryptographic limits require explicit explanation
and justified modeled evidence; they cannot silently substitute for missing
configuration or troubleshooting.

## Bash/Shell remaining path

Current305source contains77scripts in26units,680graded cases and77separate
examples under unchanged evaluator19. The prior284snapshot had74scripts/24units. New named-map and managed-configuration projects are
implemented bounded slices; broad arrays/regex, utilities, traps and safe-recovery
gaps below remain explicit. The following197–201 narrative is historical evidence,
not the current catalog or evaluator identity.

The64 integrated scripts span19 units. The60scripts in the verified installed197/553 candidate
contain477 graded cases and60 separate examples under evaluator14. Current source
contains525 graded cases and64 separate examples.
Source evaluator15 additionally supports the narrow read-only literal #/% forms;
its two interface projects are normal catalog assignments after scoped content,
registration and progress checks. Scoped native199 acceptance and the3,041-test inclusive suite pass. The existing scripting path covers bounded indexed
arrays, command-output capture, getopts, case dispatch, command-list construction,
read-only literal fallback/alternate expansion and optional-input projects,
C-locale cut/sort/uniq work, explicit pipefail, bounded case-class numeric
validation, malformed-record recovery, whole-plan publication and own-scope loop
recovery with unread-input handoff and independent first-ready file jobs.
The original agreed scope still requires the following distinct capabilities
and projects, with prerequisite teaching before assessment:

| Required area | Remaining implementation / authored work |
| --- | --- |
| Quoting and expansion | Read-only literal fallback/alternate forms and three optional-input policy/report/handover projects are implemented. Broader nested, assignment/error, substring/length/pattern operators remain unsupported; add only capabilities required by a reviewed substantive task with faithful expansion tests. |
| Arguments and interfaces | Two long-option/manifest projects are registered in199 and pass scoped grading, persistence and native checks; full-inclusive199 passes3,041 tests. Broader streamed validation and independent error handling remain required where not yet authored. |
| Functions and scope | Applied state/return/error handling across the remaining projects; broader function-call redirection/pipeline forms remain explicit subset limits unless a task needs them. Existing scope lessons are implemented. |
| Conditions and loop recovery | Two implemented assignments teach own-scope break/continue, unread-input handoff, first-success selection and independent job failures with22 graded cases. Broader conditional/regex forms and project-specific retry/lock behavior remain required where used; caller-loop and pipeline control escape remain unsupported. |
| Structured Bash | Beyond implemented bounded named-map lookup/update, map enumeration/deletion/attributes and broader conditional/regex constructs or indexed-array operations remain unsupported; new practical tasks require faithful parsing and bounds first. |
| Text processing | Three registered ERE selection/row-validation/incident-evidence projects use the bounded C-byte grep subset (installed193105phase review passed). Exact bounded UTF8 text snapshots, including final-newline differences, are now taught and graded in201. BRE, deliberate sed/awk or equivalent documented processing, binary comparison and broader log-analysis work remain required; no full regex objective credit. |
| Debugging and errors | Implemented bounded set-x decision repair, status/error recovery and owner-aware streamed log triage; broader tracing shapes, traps/cleanup and concurrency/crash recovery remain required. |
| Safe file operations | Virtual directory/path semantics, safe temporary-file/rename behavior, validated mutation, deterministic clock and lock/retry behavior where the project depends on it. |
| Substantial projects | The201 unit implements a finite preview/conditional-file rollout with complete preflight and independent old-content rollback. Later projects add bounded cleanup preview, independent file publication/recovery and finite checkpoint validation. The284project adds exact owned-setting convergence;294 adds an actual multi-file content-set/validated restore pair;305 adds attributed event analysis with reference preflight and row-level failure recovery. Broader archive/metadata backup, traps, concurrency and crash-safe resumability remain required. No atomicity, races, locks or Stop/resource rollback is claimed. |

Portable shell behavior and Bash-specific features must stay explicit. Current
[SHELL_SUBSET.md](SHELL_SUBSET.md) and per-unit references define actual behavior;
Full Bash conformance is not an agreed requirement: an unsupported construct
is not automatically a new course objective. The practical skills and projects
above remain required, with justified limits. New capabilities require
parsing/evaluation, deterministic adverse cases,
controlled development comparisons and honest saved-result migration. A capability
or test-only fixture is not a delivered lesson.
